The main loop polls {connection fd, or listen fd when unconnected, wake fd} with a timeout clamped to `POLL_MAX_MS` = 50 ms and to the idle-exit deadline, pumping `CefDoMessageLoopWork` when due with an 8 ms idle floor (`PUMP_IDLE_FLOOR_MS`) so a quiet browser doesn't spin (`web-service-main.cpp::POLL_MAX_MS`). The in-code comment records why: a fixed-interval pump starved CEF's windowless BeginFrame timer and froze animations (web-service-main.cpp::CefDoMessageLoopWork). SIGINT/SIGTERM set a stop flag (`web-service-main.cpp::requestStop`).
- **Where it lives**: `web-service-main.cpp`, additions in `BrowserApp.{cpp,h}` (`nextPumpDueMs`, `setPumpWakeFd`), `WebBrowserContext.cpp::WebBrowserContext`.
- **Surface**: None.
- **Coupling**: Internal to the new service binary; nothing upstream-shared.

### 9. CEF runtime configuration & diagnostics in `WebBrowserContext`

- **What it does**: Per-run root cache dir under the system temp dir (uuid), removed recursively in the destructor after `CefShutdown` (`WebBrowserContext.cpp::temp_directory_path`). `resources_dir_path`/`locales_dir_path` resolve relative to `/proc/self/exe`'s directory (WebBrowserContext.cpp::resources_dir_path). CEF log goes to `logs/cef/cef.log` under the state tree with 3-generation rotation through `StatePaths.h::rotate` for the daemon's service, and to `logs/cef/cef-window.log` unrotated for a windowed run's (`WebBrowserContext.cpp::LWE_DAEMON`), previously manual rotation at startup (WebBrowserContext.cpp::cefLog). `LWE_CEFLOG=verbose|info|error` adjusts severity from the `LOGSEVERITY_WARNING` default (WebBrowserContext.cpp::LWE_CEFLOG); `LWE_CEFDEBUG=<port>` enables the devtools port if 0<port<65536 (WebBrowserContext.cpp::LWE_CEFDEBUG). `CefExecuteProcess` is now only a safety net returning -1 in the browser process (WebBrowserContext.cpp::CefExecuteProcess). Windowless rendering on; `no_sandbox` still behind `CEF_NO_SANDBOX` (WebBrowserContext.cpp::CEF_NO_SANDBOX). `pumpMessageLoop()` exists (`WebBrowserContext.cpp::CefDoMessageLoopWork`) but the service's own loop calls `CefDoMessageLoopWork` directly.
- **Where it lives**: Modified upstream file `src/WallpaperEngine/WebBrowser/WebBrowserContext.{cpp,h}`.
- **Surface**: env `LWE_CEFLOG` (WebBrowserContext.cpp::LWE_CEFLOG), `LWE_CEFDEBUG` (WebBrowserContext.cpp::LWE_CEFDEBUG); log path fixed under `$HOME` (WebBrowserContext.cpp::cefLog).
- **Coupling**: Single file, service-target only; the engine no longer includes it.

### 10. `CWeb` rewritten as an shm/texture client
- **Frame rate**: the helper paints at the engine's FPS cap clamped to CEF's 1..60, decided once in the engine and passed through (CWeb.cpp::maximumFPS); it used to take the larger of 60 and the cap.

- **What it does**: Upstream's `CWeb` (123 lines) held `CefBrowser`/`BrowserClient`/`RenderHandler` in-process. The fork's (257 lines) holds an `InstanceId`, a `HelperClient&` and a `FrameReader` (`CWeb.h::InstanceId`). Per frame it resizes on viewport change, injects properties once after page-loaded, forwards gated mouse moves/clicks, consumes the shm ring into `glTexSubImage2D` (texture realloc centralized in `allocateTexture`, the only `glTexImage2D`, `CWeb.cpp::allocateTexture`), and sends audio every other frame when the project supports it (`CWeb.cpp::injectAudio`). It tracks the async resize round-trip by re-deriving texture size from the *buffer's* geometry rather than the viewport (`CWeb.cpp::glTexSubImage2D`). Pointer moves go through the new `Input::PointerMoveGate` (`CWeb.cpp::update`) - a separate module with its own test (`Testing/Cases/PointerMoveGate.cpp`). Mouse buttons are Left/Right only (`Protocol.h::MouseButton`).
- **Where it lives**: Rewritten upstream files `src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Wallpapers/CWeb.{cpp,h}`.
- **Surface**: env `LWE_MOUSEDBG` (any set value), read once, enables a 1/s mouse-trail log (`CWeb.cpp::LWE_MOUSEDBG`).
- **Coupling**: Depends on capabilities 3, 4, 6; the `fromWallpaper` signature change is the only upstream-shared touchpoint.

### 11. `lwe-web-frame-probe` diagnostics/verification tool

- **What it does**: A standalone BUILD_TESTING-only executable (`CMakeLists.txt::lwe-web-frame-probe`) that acts as a fake engine: spawns a real service via `HelperClient`, then runs one of three proofs - frame transport (default; sequence monotonicity, tear-free reads under a forced writer-lap stall, resize generation change), `--verbs` (per-verb behavioral assertions measured as frame-delta over a quiet baseline with a 0.10 floor: clicks, moves, live set-property typing, audio, resize), or `--lifecycle` (teardown-to-zero, SIGKILL respawn with property replay, crash-loop guard timing, then a census of `/proc` comms and `/dev/shm` for orphans). CLI: `--wallpaper=<dir>` (required), `--assets=`, `--seconds=`, `--width/--height/--resize-width/--resize-height/--fps=`, `--verbs|--lifecycle`, `--crashguard=`, `--idle-exit=` (the last two setenv the corresponding env vars, tools/web-frame-probe.cpp::LWE_WEB_IDLE_EXIT_MS).
- **Where it lives**: New file `tools/web-frame-probe.cpp` (1695 lines).
- **Coupling**: None - links only `linux-wallpaperengine-lib`.

---

### Five-line summary

The fork removes CEF from the engine entirely: upstream's in-process `WebBrowserContext` is replaced by a three-process design where the engine (which no longer links libcef) posix_spawns a new `lwe-web-service` binary on first web wallpaper, and that service owns CEF while a tiny `lwe-web-helper` binary hosts CEF's child processes. Frames travel over a versioned, double-slot seqlock ring in per-instance POSIX shm objects (`/dev/shm/lwe-web-<pid>-<id>-<gen>`) that the engine maps read-only, with control (create/resize/mouse/typed properties/audio/destroy, plus loaded/failed/frame-ready events) over a same-uid-authenticated, permission-pinned Unix socket with 1 MiB payload and 8 MiB queue caps. The `wp<workshopId>` scheme universe is pre-enumerated from the wallpaper library and baked into the hardened spawn config (close-from-3, restored signal mask, protocol-version handshake). Lifecycle is fully supervised: the service idle-exits 1 s after the last instance (`LWE_WEB_IDLE_EXIT_MS`), the engine detects deaths, unlinks orphan shm, replays instance+property state into a respawned helper, and a crash-loop guard (`LWE_WEB_CRASHGUARD`) imposes cooldowns. Coverage is two Catch2 cases proving zero-cost startup for non-web sessions (`WebHelperStartupCost.cpp`) plus a ~1700-line integration probe (`tools/web-frame-probe.cpp`) that measures frame transport, per-verb behavior, respawn fidelity and orphan hygiene; upstream coupling is concentrated in the `CWallpaper::fromWallpaper` signature, `WallpaperApplication` wiring, and a rewritten `CWeb`.

---

# 3. Render core, VRAM and the texture pipeline


### FBO pooling model (pooled ping-pong composite pairs)
- **What it does**: Layer composite FBOs (`_rt_imageLayerComposite_<id>_a/_b`) are leased from a per-scene pool keyed by `WxH_flags_format` instead of being dedicated per layer (`CScene::leaseCompositePair`, CScene.cpp::leaseCompositePair). FBOs whose names are referenced cross-object (found by `collectSharedComposites` scanning every image's effect targets/sources/binds/overrides, CScene.cpp::collectSharedComposites) stay dedicated, as does the hardcoded bloom composite id (CScene.cpp::m_sharedCompositeIds) id -1 (CScene.cpp::collectSharedComposites). Same-size layers share one a/b pair; `reportPoolHighWater` logs reuse stats (CScene.cpp::reportPoolHighWater). Invisible layers still render when their composite is shared (`isCompositeShared` gate, CImage.cpp::isCompositeShared).
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/Wallpapers/CScene.{h,cpp}` (CScene.h::m_poolDedicatedCount pool members, CScene.cpp::leaseCompositePair lease) with the sole consumer at `Render/Objects/CImage.cpp::leaseCompositePair`.
- **Surface**: `LWE_FBOPOOL=0` disables pooling (exact legacy per-layer allocation; CScene.cpp::LWE_FBOPOOL; default ON). `LWE_POOL_HWM=1` enables high-water logging (CScene.cpp::LWE_POOL_HWM).
- **Coupling**: Small surface but semantics-touching: three new CScene methods + members, one CImage call site, plus the shared-composite name scan. Pooled FBOs also interact with the composition-subtree render path below. A porter takes CScene pool members/methods + the CImage lease block; the pool is safe to drop entirely (fallback branch is the legacy path).
- **Tests**: none.

### Coverage-sized composite FBOs + output-derived supersample clamp (canvas/view split)
- **What it does**: Layer composite FBOs are sized to the layer's on-screen coverage (`max(authored size, min(|size*scale|, scene dims))`) instead of the authored size (CImage.cpp::LWE_FBOCOVERAGE), so scaled-up layers don't upscale from a tiny FBO. The scene FBO and composition-layer FBOs then pass through `CScene::clampToCap` (CScene.cpp::clampToCap), which aspect-preservingly clamps to `largestOutputSize() * LWE_SSFACTOR` (the largest single screen the wallpaper plays on, not the multi-output span: the largest live viewport on the desktop, and for a window the largest monitor rather than the window itself, so a bench window clamps the way the desktop does, CScene.cpp::largestOutputSize, GLFWWindowOutput.cpp::largestScreenSize; `LWE_CLAMPOUTPUT=WxH` overrides it for measurement, CScene.cpp::parseOutputSize), with fill/default modes clamped to *cover* the cap and the rest to *fit inside* it. Layer composites and effect-pass targets take the same rule through `CScene::clampToEffectCap` (CScene.cpp::clampToEffectCap) with their own factor, `LWE_CLAMPCOMPOSITES`, a number like the scene factor: default 1, at most 4, and 0 or below exempts them (Application/Config.cpp::LWE_CLAMPCOMPOSITES). Every effect pass resamples the whole layer through them, so a chain shrunk to the output size blurs by its texel size (ten displacement passes at a third of the authored size read as a soft layer next to upstream), and with the exemption the layer takes one downsample into the scene target instead. Measured on that scene in a 1280x720 window: clamped 165 MB at 0.17 of upstream's sharpness, exempt 249 MB at 0.98, unclamped 283 MB. The default keeps the cheaper trade. Separately, the fork decouples canvas dims (`m_canvasWidth/Height`, CScene.h::m_canvasWidth) from camera view dims: camera zoom no longer resizes the scene FBO (comment CScene.cpp::dims; `getWidth/getHeight` now return canvas, CScene.cpp::getWidth).
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/Wallpapers/CScene.{h,cpp}`, `Render/Objects/CImage.cpp` (coverage block CImage.cpp::coverage, effect-FBO clamp at CImage.cpp::clampToEffectCap), `Render/CWallpaper.{h,cpp}` (`clampToCap` virtual default identity at CWallpaper.h::clampToCap; scene FBO clamped in `setupFramebuffers`, CWallpaper.cpp::setupFramebuffers).
- **Surface**: `LWE_SSFACTOR` float, read by Config, default 1.0, `0` disables the clamp, a value above 4 reads as 4 (Application/Config.cpp::LWE_SSFACTOR). `LWE_NOFBOCOVERAGE` disables coverage sizing (default ON; CImage.cpp::LWE_NOFBOCOVERAGE). `LWE_FBOCOVERAGE` logs coverage upsizes (CImage.cpp::LWE_FBOCOVERAGE). A show's `ssfactor` and `clampcomposites` set the scene factor and the effect factor per scene at load, each a number no greater than 4 where 0 or below is off; the launch value (the flag, else the environment) is the default when the show says nothing, an environment value above 4 reading as 4 (Render/LoadQuality.cpp::ssfactor, CScene.cpp::m_ssfactor, CScene.cpp::m_clampComposites). The values persist with the lane look and replay at boot (Lane.cpp::texdetail), and a persisted `res` word loads as the two numbers: `screen` as 1 and 1, `sharpfx` as 1 and 0, `wallpaper` as 0 and 0 (Lane.cpp::migrateRes).
- **Coupling**: Woven through the render frame: the scene FBO and composition FBOs route through `clampToCap` with the scene factor (CImage.cpp::clampToCap), layer composites and effect pass targets through `clampToEffectCap` (CImage.cpp::clampToEffectCap) with the effect factor, `LWE_CLAMPCOMPOSITES`, a number at most 4 with 0 or below off; the screenshot reader sizes its read by the scene target's real size rather than the canvas, since the two differ under the cap (WallpaperApplication.cpp::takeScreenshot); and the scaling mode read from `WallpaperState` must agree with the present-pass UV logic. A porter needs CScene's `clampToCap`/`clampToEffectCap`/`largestOutputSize`/`m_canvas*`, the CWallpaper virtual + setupFramebuffers change, and the CImage coverage block.
- **Tests**: `Cases/ClampOutput.cpp` covers the override's grammar.

### Composition-layer subtree rendering
- **What it does**: Image layers flagged as composition layers with authored children get a scene-sized `_rt_compositionLayer_<id>` FBO that aliases `_rt_FullFrameBuffer` for their subtree (CImage.cpp::_rt_FullFrameBuffer). `CScene::renderFrame` walks each object's parent chain (`compositionAncestor`), renders the whole subtree into the composite FBO (with optional `glBlitFramebuffer` background copy for `copiesCompositionBackground`), recurses into nested composition layers, then renders the composite itself (CScene.cpp::copiesCompositionBackground, lambda `renderCompositionImpl` at CScene.cpp::renderCompositionImpl). `m_compositionRenderTarget` + `getActiveRenderTarget/resolveRenderTarget` (CScene.cpp::m_compositionRenderTarget) redirect "the scene FBO" references while inside a subtree. `createObject` gained cycle protection (`m_objectsBeingResolved` + RAII guard, CScene.cpp::m_objectsBeingResolved) since dependency/parent cycles now terminate with an error instead of infinite recursion.
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/Wallpapers/CScene.{h,cpp}` and `Render/Objects/CImage.{h,cpp}` (the CImage side is the other half of the change).
- **Surface**: no dedicated env knobs; honors the existing `render.debug.objectFilter`/`skipObjects` settings (gate `enabledByDebug`, CScene.cpp::enabledByDebug) and `LWE_SKIPGATE=0` to bypass the constructor-time skip gate (CScene.cpp::LWE_SKIPGATE).
- **Coupling**: Deeply woven into `CScene::renderFrame` and `CImage` construction. Cherry-picking means taking the renderFrame restructure plus CImage's composition-FBO/alias logic together.
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: exact semantics of `copiesCompositionBackground()` and `isCompositionLayer()` are defined in CImage; only the call sites are checked here, not the predicate bodies.

### HDR bloom ladder
- **What it does**: When `camera.bloom.enabled && camera.bloom.hdr` (CScene.cpp::hdrLadder), the scene FBO switches to `TextureFormat_RGBA16161616f` (m_sceneFormat, CScene.cpp::TextureFormat_RGBA16161616f; CWallpaper.h::m_sceneFormat, consumed at CWallpaper.cpp::setupFramebuffers) and CFBO allocates it as `GL_RGBA16F` (CFBO.cpp::TextureFormat_RGBA16161616f). The fork generates a mip-ladder bloom effect at load: N RGBA16F half-resolution targets `_rt_hdrBloom_1..N` (N = `bloomhdriterations`, clamp 1..12), a prefilter pass with soft-knee blend params + strength normalization `authored/(1+scatter^(N-2))`, down passes, additive up passes with scatter, and a combine pass blending `_rt_imageLayerComposite_-1_a` with level 1 back into `_rt_FullFrameBuffer` (CScene.cpp::TextureFormat_RGBA16161616f). Ladder sizes derive from the largest output, not the canvas (CScene.cpp::largestOutputSize). The effect JSON is injected into the project VFS at runtime (CScene.cpp::getVFS); the materials/shaders (`materials/wpelinux/hdr_*.json`, `shaders/wpelinux_hdr_downsample.*`, `wpelinux_combine_hdr.*`) are injected earlier by `Assets/AssetLocator.cpp::g_TexelSize`, which string-patches `g_RenderVar0`/`g_TexelSize` declarations into material-annotated uniforms. Layer composites also become float under HDR bloom so pre-clamp >1 values survive (`isHdrBloom`, CScene.h::isHdrBloom; CImage.cpp::isHdrBloom). Non-HDR bloom additionally gains `bloomtint` (CScene.cpp::bloomTintVec).
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/Wallpapers/CScene.cpp` (ctor, ~240 lines), `Render/CFBO.cpp` (RGBA16F allocation), `Render/CWallpaper.{h,cpp}` (m_sceneFormat), `Assets/AssetLocator.cpp` (shader/material VFS injection). Data fields in `Data/Model/Wallpaper.h::bloom`, parsed at `Data/Parsers/WallpaperParser.cpp::parseScene`.
- **Surface**: wallpaper-authored properties: `hdr`, `bloomhdriterations` (default 8), `bloomhdrscatter` (1.619), `bloomhdrfeather` (0.1), `bloomhdrstrength` (2.0), `bloomhdrthreshold` (1.0), `bloomtint` (WallpaperParser.cpp::bloomtint). `LWE_NOBLOOM` env kills all bloom including the HDR ladder (CScene.cpp::LWE_NOBLOOM).
- **Coupling**: Touches CFBO format handling, CWallpaper FBO creation, CScene ctor, CImage composite format, AssetLocator VFS injection, and the Wallpaper data model. A porter needs all six; the ladder itself is confined to the CScene ctor block and could be excised as a unit if the data-model fields come along.
- **Tests**: none.

### BC7/BC4/BC5 texture ingest (offline texcache)
- **What it does**: A standalone encoder shim `tools/texcomp/lwe_bc7enc.cpp` wraps vendored ISPCTextureCompressor: stdin `[u32 w][u32 h][u32 fmt(7=BC7,4=BC4,5=BC5)][RGBA8 pixels]`, stdout raw compressed blocks, edge-replicated padding to 4x4 multiples, BC7 at max quality (`GetProfile_alpha_slow`, lwe_bc7enc.cpp::GetProfile_alpha_slow). Built only on x86-64 when `ispc` is found (CMakeLists.txt::lwe_bc7enc). At texture load, `uploadFromTexcache` (CTexture.cpp::uploadFromTexcache) looks up `$HOME/.local/state/lwe/engine/texcache/<sha256-of-stored-mip0>`: the `.meta` JSON contract is `{"gl": "BC7"|"BC4"|"BC5", "mips": [[w,h,bytes],...]}` and the `.bc` blob is the concatenated mip levels in raster order; eligible formats are ARGB8888->BC7, R8->BC4, RG88->BC5, single-image non-animated textures only (CTexture.cpp::uploadFromTexcache). On hit it uploads via `glCompressedTexImage2D` with mip-residency rebasing applied (startLevel logic, CTexture.cpp::glCompressedTexImage2D); on any mismatch it silently falls back to the normal path.
- **Where it lives**: new file `tools/texcomp/lwe_bc7enc.cpp`; new function in modified `Render/CTexture.cpp`; CMake wiring CMakeLists.txt::lwe_bc7enc (the only `src/External/` entanglement: ISPC kernel compiled from `src/External/ISPCTextureCompressor/ispc_texcomp`).
- **Surface**: `--texturecache off`, or `LWE_TEXCOMP=0` when no flag is given, disables the cache lookup (read by Config, Application/Config.cpp::LWE_TEXCOMP); `LWE_SRGBALL` also forces fallback (CTexture.cpp::LWE_SRGBALL). Cache location is hardcoded `$HOME/.local/state/lwe/engine/texcache` (CTexture.cpp::uploadFromTexcache). A show's `texcomp` bool decides the lookup per scene (Render/LoadQuality.cpp::texcomp, CTexture.cpp::allowCache), and the texture cache keys a raw request apart from a compressed one so neither is handed the other's upload (TextureCache.cpp::cacheKey); the album art pair keeps its plain name under every choice. A texture that was uploaded raw under the plain key (compression on, no cache file) is uploaded again for a raw request; the pair coexists only for cache survivors, since a rebuild evicts before it builds. The look and status carry texcomp as the string "1" / "0".
- **Coupling**: Self-contained on the engine side (one function + one call site at CTexture.cpp::createGL) but depends on the fork's LZ4-retained mip model (`materializeMip`, CTexture.cpp::materializeMip) to hash the stored mip0 bytes, after LZ4 and before any image decode. A porter could lift it with a plain RGBA readback in place of `materializeMip`.
- **Validation**: the meta file's mip list is checked before use and an entry that does not describe one falls back to the raw asset, which is always present (CTexture.cpp::uploadFromTexcache); an image the decoder rejects stops the chain with a logged error instead of an upload of undefined contents; the producer writes the meta through a temp file and a rename like the blob, so a failed write fails the encode (`texcomp.py`).
- **Tests**: `lwe-ui/tests/test_texcomp.py` covers the producer side of the cache contract (eligibility, key, blob layout, meta fields, cache-hit no-op, cancellation); the C++ shim and the `CTexture` read path have none.
- **Producer**: `lwe-ui/src/lwe_ui/texcomp.py` writes the cache in-tree. It resolves the `lwe_bc7enc` shim, invokes it per mip level, and writes the `.bc`/`.meta` pair under the same cache root with the same key the engine reads.

### Mip-residency live-cap and demand expansion
- **What it does**: On by default, and turned off by `--texturedetail full`, or when no flag is given by `LWE_TEXDETAIL` set to any value other than `auto`, empty included (Application/Config.cpp::LWE_TEXDETAIL), the fork caps texture uploads so the largest resident mip dimension ~ the largest live output dimension. At scene construction `buildReferenceMap` walks the data-model scene and records a per-texture verdict: cappable only if every consuming pass uses one of six allowlisted shaders (genericimage*, generic4, chroma4) and the object has no animation layers and isn't perspective (MipResidency.cpp::kAllowlist); verdicts are process-global and monotonically demote because the TextureCache outlives scenes (comment MipResidency.cpp::TextureCache). At resolve time `TextureCache` computes `capDim = capDimension(largestOutputDimension(context))` - 0 (full chain) whenever `MipResidency::cappable(filename)` is false, and otherwise queried live from current viewports, hotplug-fresh - and passes it to the new `CTexture` ctor arg (TextureCache.cpp::CTexture). CTexture then skips stored levels above the cap and uploads the remainder rebased to GL level 0 with tightened `GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL`, on both the raw path (CTexture.cpp::GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL) and the BC7 texcache path (CTexture.cpp::GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL). Per frame, `CImage::render` calls `MipResidency::maybeExpand` with the object's on-canvas quad size (CImage.cpp::maybeExpand); a quad exceeding 1.02xcap triggers `expandCappedTexture` -> `CTexture::expandResidency`, which deletes the capped GL object and re-uploads the full chain from retained RAM (MipResidency.cpp::maybeExpand; CTexture.cpp::expandResidency).
- **Where it lives**: new module `Render/MipResidency.{h,cpp}`; wiring in modified `Render/TextureCache.cpp` (TextureCache.cpp::chain), `Render/CTexture.{h,cpp}` (ctor capDimension, startLevel logic), call site in `Render/Objects/CImage.cpp::maybeExpand`, map build in `Render/Wallpapers/CScene.cpp::buildReferenceMap`.
- **Surface**: on unless `--texturedetail full`, or when no flag is given `LWE_TEXDETAIL` set to any value other than `auto` (empty included), turns it off, which makes it fully inert (read by Config, Application/Config.cpp::LWE_TEXDETAIL). `LWE_TEXCAP=<int>=256>` is a test-only cap override (MipResidency.cpp::LWE_TEXCAP). `LWE_MIPRESIDENCY_DEBUG` logs verdicts (MipResidency.cpp::LWE_MIPRESIDENCY_DEBUG). Default cap fallback is 4096 when no live output size is available (MipResidency.cpp::capDimension). A show's `texdetail` (auto / full) sets the switch at scene load (MipResidency.cpp::setEnabled, Render/LoadQuality.cpp::texdetailAuto), engine-wide from the last scene loaded, so a video or web show carrying `texdetail` leaves the switch where the last scene set it; a full-chain request is keyed apart in the texture cache (TextureCache.cpp::cacheKey).
- **Coupling**: The module itself is small and self-contained, but it threads a new ctor parameter through TextureCache->CTexture and relies on retained mip payloads (LZ4) for expansion. The per-frame hook is one call in CImage. Lifting it requires: MipResidency.*, the TextureCache 4-line block, CTexture's startLevel/rebase logic in both upload paths, and the CImage hook.
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: expansion deletes and recreates the GL texture object, so holders must re-query ids per bind (the contract at CTexture.h::stream); holders of `GLuint` texture ids outside `Render/` have not all been audited against that swap.

### LZ4-backed RAM slimming of retained pixels
- **What it does**: `materializeMip` (CTexture.cpp::materializeMip) treats `Mipmap::compressedData` (LZ4 block retained by the parser; upstream already kept it for standard .tex, the fork adds the raw-GL path, `Data/Parsers/TextureParser.cpp::compressedData`) as the canonical backing store, decoding on demand. After upload, `slimRetainedPixels` (CTexture.cpp::slimRetainedPixels) frees the uncompressed copy wherever both exist - skipping video-backed textures since the player streams from the retained buffer - so idle RAM holds only the LZ4 blocks, which are what `expandResidency` re-uploads from.
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/CTexture.cpp` (CTexture.cpp::MipPixels, slim call at CTexture.cpp::slimRetainedPixels); the producer half lives in `Data/Parsers/TextureParser.cpp` (covered separately).
- **Surface**: none (no knobs); logs `LWE-RAMSLIM`.
- **Coupling**: Depends on the retained LZ4 backing (kept by upstream for standard .tex, extended by the fork's parser for raw-GL); without `compressedData` populated it is a no-op.
- **Tests**: none.

### Texture cache eviction
- **What it does**: `TextureCache::evictUnused()` (TextureCache.cpp::LWE_TEXCACHEDUMP) erases every cache entry whose `shared_ptr` use_count is 1 (i.e. the cache is the sole owner), returning the count. Called at two natural quiet points via `RenderContext::evictUnusedTextures` (RenderContext.cpp::evictUnusedTextures): `rebuildForCurrentBackgrounds` after clearing wallpapers (WallpaperApplication.cpp::rebuildForCurrentBackgrounds) and `apiReleaseOutputs` during output release (WallpaperApplication.cpp::clearWallpapers). `RenderContext::clearWallpapers` (RenderContext.cpp::clearWallpapers) supports the same teardown.
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/TextureCache.{h,cpp}` and `Render/RenderContext.{h,cpp}`; call sites in WallpaperApplication.
- **Surface**: `LWE_TEXCACHEDUMP` logs survivors with refcount (TextureCache.cpp::LWE_TEXCACHEDUMP) plus wallpaper ctor/dtor life logs (CWallpaper.cpp::LWE_TEXCACHEDUMP).
- **Coupling**: Trivially liftable - one method plus three call sites.
- **Tests**: none.

### Camera / projection corrections
- **What it does**: (a) New `setPerspectiveProjection` (Camera.cpp::setPerspectiveProjection) for scenes without an ortho projection block (`isPerspective` = null projection JSON, WallpaperParser.cpp::is_null): Y-flipped `glm::perspective` with clamped near/far, authored eye/center/up as the runtime lookAt, and a separate ortho `m_screenProjection` for screen-space layout, exposed via `getScreenProjection` (Camera.cpp::getScreenProjection) and consumed by CImage/CText (CImage.cpp::getScreenProjection, CText.cpp::getScreenProjection). (b) `setOrthogonalProjection` rewritten (Camera.cpp::setOrthogonalProjection): honors `general.zoom` (view extent = canvas/zoom, zoom<=0 clamped to 1), symmetric +/-halfRange z-range (max(farz,1000)), identity lookAt - dropping upstream's `glm::translate(projection, eye)`. (c) `setScriptedView` (Camera.cpp::setScriptedView) lets scripts override eye/center with NaN/degenerate guards, feeding getEye/getCenter (Camera.cpp::getCenter); called from `Scripting/SceneObject.cpp::setScriptedView`. (d) `getOverrideFov` exposes `perspectiveoverridefov` (Camera.cpp::getOverrideFov; used by CImage.cpp::getOverrideFov and CModel.cpp::getOverrideFov).
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/Camera.{h,cpp}`; selection logic in `CScene.cpp::setPerspectiveProjection`.
- **Surface**: wallpaper data keys `general.zoom` / `perspectiveoverridefov` / null `general.orthogonalprojection`; `LWE_CAMPROBE` env logs scripted views (Camera.cpp::LWE_CAMPROBE).
- **Coupling**: Camera.cpp is small and liftable as a unit, but consumers (CImage/CText/CModel MVP construction, CScene ctor) must come along; ortho behavior change (zoom, no eye-translate) alters every 2D scene's framing, so it's not drop-in for upstream without the canvas/view split.
- **Tests**: none.

### OverlayLabel debug overlay
- **What it does**: Every present pass draws a multi-line text label in one corner of each viewport, white over a one pixel black outline so it reads on light scenes (OverlayLabel.cpp::draw). The text, corner and visibility are live state (OverlayLabel.cpp::set, OverlayLabel.h::Settings): `LWE_OVERLAY_TEXT` seeds the text once unless a command set it first (OverlayLabel.cpp::readEnvironment), and the `set-overlay` verb changes any of the three on a running engine (WallpaperApplication.cpp::set-overlay). Text is rasterized with FreeType into an R8 texture whenever it changes (OverlayLabel.cpp::refresh): the first line at the heading size, 40 px by default, every later line at two thirds of it (OverlayLabel.cpp::kBodyScale), from `LWE_OVERLAY_FONT` or the built-in DejaVu/Liberation path list (OverlayLabel.cpp::kFonts), with `LWE_OVERLAY_SIZE` overriding the heading size (OverlayLabel.cpp::loadFont); drawn scaled to the viewport, 2160 px tall = full size, floor 0.5 (OverlayLabel.cpp::kReferenceHeight). The font face stays open for the process. GL setup failure is latched so a broken driver never retries per frame (OverlayLabel.cpp::init); a text that fails to rasterize hides the overlay until the text changes. GL state (program/VAO/texture/blend) is saved and restored around the draw.
- **Where it lives**: new module `Render/OverlayLabel.{h,cpp}`; single call site in `Render/CWallpaper.cpp::draw` (end of `CWallpaper::render`); verb handler in `WallpaperApplication.cpp::set-overlay`, validation in `Api/CommandDispatcher.cpp::parse`. `status` reports `overlay.visible` and `overlay.corner` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::apiStatus).
- **Surface**: `LWE_OVERLAY_TEXT` env var, unset/empty = nothing drawn until a command supplies text (OverlayLabel.cpp::LWE_OVERLAY_TEXT); `set-overlay` with any of `text` (<=512 printable ASCII chars and newlines), `corner` (top-left/top-right/bottom-left/bottom-right), `visible` (bool).
- **Coupling**: Self-contained; porter takes the two files plus one call line, and the verb if live control is wanted. Reuses the project's existing FreeType dependency; no new link is introduced.
- **Tests**: `Testing/Cases/CommandDispatcher.cpp::set-overlay` covers the verb's validation.

### AlbumTexture readback fix
- **What it does**: `AlbumTexture::copyContents` switches the GPU->CPU readback from `glGetnTexImage` (GL 4.5) to `glGetTexImage` (GL 1.0) (AlbumTexture.cpp::copyContents). The in-code comment states the reason: the engine targets a 3.3 core context and the GLFW/--window driver doesn't set `glewExperimental`, so the 4.5 function pointer is NULL -> SIGSEGV on every MPRIS album-art update.
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/AlbumTexture.cpp` only (AlbumTexture.h untouched).
- **Surface**: none.
- **Coupling**: One-line lift; trivially cherry-pickable.
- **Tests**: none.

### Driver-level changes (GLFW + factories)
- **What it does**: `GLFWOpenGLDriver`: MSAA samples 4->0 (GLFWOpenGLDriver.cpp::GLFW_SAMPLES); frame-time cap recomputed every pass from `settings.render.maximumFPS` so live `set-fps` takes effect (GLFWOpenGLDriver.cpp::dispatchEventQueue, vs upstream's one-time static); window title overridable via `LWE_WINTITLE` (GLFWOpenGLDriver.cpp::LWE_WINTITLE). `VideoDriver.h` gains `canReleaseOutputSurfaces()`/`releaseOutputSurfaces()`/`acquireOutputSurfaces()` virtuals defaulting false (VideoDriver.h::canReleaseOutputSurfaces, VideoDriver.h::releaseOutputSurfaces), implemented only by the Wayland driver (`WaylandOpenGLDriver.h::canReleaseOutputSurfaces`, `WaylandOpenGLDriver.cpp::releaseOutputSurfaces`) and consumed by `apiReleaseOutputs` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::apiReleaseOutputs); `canReleaseOutputSurfaces` is read by `apiReleaseOutputs`, `tickDeadman`, `fullscreenBehaviorInEffect` and `tickAppCondition` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::tickDeadman, WallpaperApplication.cpp::fullscreenBehaviorInEffect, WallpaperApplication.cpp::tickAppCondition). `VideoFactories.cpp::createFullscreenDetector`: with the command socket on (`apiSocket`, which `--daemon` also sets) the fullscreen detector factory is used even when `pauseOnFullscreen` is off (`Application/FullscreenPolicy.cpp::wantsRealDetector`). The X11 path is release-safe: the fullscreen detector queries the real X11 window (X11FullScreenDetector.cpp::glfwGetX11Window), the output frees its image once and guards every release (X11Output.cpp::haveImageBuffer), and the driver deletes its output on teardown (GLFWOpenGLDriver.cpp::glfwTerminate).
- **Where it lives**: modified upstream files listed above; the Wayland implementation is covered elsewhere.
- **Surface**: `LWE_WINTITLE` env (GLFWOpenGLDriver.cpp::LWE_WINTITLE); existing `settings.render.maximumFPS`, `settings.render.pauseOnFullscreen`, `settings.general.apiSocket`.
- **Coupling**: Each hunk is independent and small; the surface-release pair needs the Wayland implementation plus the apiReleaseOutputs caller to mean anything.
- **Tests**: none.

### Present-pass fit window (zoom and pan inside the scaling mode)
- **What it does**: `WallpaperState` carries a `Fit {zoom, panX, panY}` (WallpaperState.h::Fit) that narrows the UV window the scaling mode computed: the visible half-span shrinks by the zoom and the centre moves by the pan as a fraction of half the visible span at that zoom, so a full pan slides the picture half a screen at any zoom (a quarter of the picture at zoom 2); the picture follows the pan, +x toward the viewport's right and +y toward its top whether or not v is flipped, so the sampled window moves the other way (WallpaperState.cpp::applyFit). It runs at the end of `updateState` (WallpaperState.cpp::updateState), and a requested fit that differs from the applied one counts as a change in `hasChanged` (WallpaperState.cpp::hasChanged), so the next present recomputes with no rebuild. Because the window lives in the cached UVs, the span slice in `CWallpaper::render` (CWallpaper.cpp::render), the mouse mapping in `CScene::updateMouse` (CScene.cpp::updateMouse) and the screenshot capture all inherit it. Two layers compose: the wallpaper layer from the show's `fit` arg or `set-fit {layer: "wallpaper"}` (which also writes the current show record, so `prev` and a restart keep it) and the lane layer from `set-fit` or `lanes-set`; zooms multiply, pans add, the result is clamped to 1..2 and -1..1 (WallpaperState.cpp::composeFit). The zoom is inward only; zooming out is what the scaling modes are for. At zoom 1 the pan carries the window past the picture's edge and the sampler's clamp mode fills the rest, held edge pixels or the border colour; the chip reads percent of the screen, plus or minus fifty at the ends.
- **Where it lives**: `Render/WallpaperState.{h,cpp}` (`Fit`, `setFit`, `applyFit`, `composeFit`), `Render/CWallpaper.{h,cpp}::setFit` (CWallpaper.cpp::setFit), `Api/Lane.h`: the wallpaper layer is `Look::fit` (Lane.h::Look), written by `applyShowCore` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::applyShowCore) before the rebuild and rolled back with the rest of the show; `effectiveFit` composes a lane's two layers (WallpaperApplication.cpp::effectiveFit); `applyFitWindow` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::applyFitWindow) is called at the end of `buildWallpapers`, after a legacy playlist advance (which resets the wallpaper layer to identity), after state restore whether or not the restore completed, by `apiSetFit` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::apiSetFit) and by `apiLanesSet` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::apiLanesSet). Both layers persist in the state file with the lane (Lane.cpp::fit, Lane.cpp::look) through `fitFromJson` (Lane.cpp::fitFromJson), which defaults a missing or mistyped value and clamps an out-of-range one, so a hand-edited state file cannot put an unclamped window on screen; the wallpaper layer also rides the show record, so `prev` and a restart restore it with the show.
- **Surface**: show arg `fit`, verb `set-fit` (`layer` picks the wallpaper or lane layer, `WallpaperApplication.cpp::apiSetFit`), status `fit` and `fit_effective`; panel keys `FIT_ZOOM`, `FIT_PAN_X`, `FIT_PAN_Y` (constants.py::FIT_ZOOM), resolved into every show and playlist entry by `resolve_fit` (resolve.py::resolve_fit), client `api_client.set_fit` (api_client.py::set_fit).
- **Coupling**: confined to `WallpaperState` plus one setter on `CWallpaper`; the app side is three small functions and one field. Lifts with the state class.
- **Tests**: `Cases/FitWindow.cpp` - identity is a no-op, zoom 2 windows and the quarter travel, the flipped v direction, nesting inside a fill crop, input clamping, layer composition, and the mouse mapping arithmetic on the same window (Cases/FitWindow.cpp). Panel: `test_storage.py` round-trips the keys, `test_engine_sync.py` pins the resolved `fit` on shows and playlist entries.

### Present-pass color correction + sRGB experiments
- **What it does**: The wallpaper present shader (inline in `CWallpaper::setupShaders`) gains `g_CC` (brightness/contrast/saturation/hue-rotation) and `g_SrgbOut` (gamma 1/2.2) uniforms (CWallpaper.cpp::g_SrgbOut), fed per present from `app.getColorCorrection()` (CWallpaper.cpp::getColorCorrection). Companion sRGB internal-format experiments: `LWE_SRGBALBEDO` maps DXT5 to sRGB and forces sRGB output; `LWE_SRGBALL` maps all albedo formats to sRGB (CTexture.cpp::LWE_SRGBALL, CWallpaper.cpp::g_CC). Also in CWallpaper: mirror-group decode ownership (`setMirrorOwner` gates `renderFrame` to the named screen, CWallpaper.cpp::renderFrame; when the owner's output goes away the driver names the departed screen before freeing its viewport and the application hands ownership to a member that still has a live viewport, or clears it, so the mirrors keep rendering, Render/MirrorOwner.h::next) and a hardcoded `LWE-PRESENT` diagnostic log for the first 3 frames (CWallpaper.cpp::LWE_PRESENTTRACE, now env-gated behind `LWE_PRESENTTRACE` and emitted at out-level).
- **Where it lives**: modified `Render/CWallpaper.{h,cpp}`, `Render/CTexture.cpp`.
- **Surface**: `LWE_SRGBALBEDO`, `LWE_SRGBALL` envs; color-correction values come from application settings (getColorCorrection - the settings/API side is covered elsewhere).
- **Coupling**: Shader change is confined to CWallpaper; sRGB format mapping touches `setupInternalFormat` and `uploadFromTexcache`, which bails out of the compressed path under `LWE_SRGBALL` so the format switch takes effect. Both easily liftable.
- **Tests**: none.

### Render debug instrumentation (consolidated)
Env-gated diagnostics added across the render frame, all default-off read via `getenv`: `LWE_FBOALLOC` (FBOProvider.cpp::LWE_FBOALLOC), `LWE_FBOTRACE` (FBOProvider.cpp::LWE_FBOTRACE), `LWE_AUDIT` (TextureCache.cpp::LWE_AUDIT; GPU readback stats CTexture.cpp::LWE_AUDIT), `LWE_MASKAUDIT` (CTexture.cpp::LWE_MASKAUDIT), `LWE_BASELEVEL_PROBE` (CTexture.cpp::LWE_BASELEVEL_PROBE; runs a one-time driver behavior probe, baseLevelProbe CTexture.cpp::baseLevelProbe), `LWE_CLEARPROBE` (CScene.cpp::LWE_CLEARPROBE), `LWE_LEDGER` (CScene.cpp::LWE_LEDGER), `LWE_OBJPROBE` + `LWE_OBJPROBE_FLOAT` (CScene.cpp::LWE_OBJPROBE), `LWE_FBDUMP` + `LWE_FBDUMP_FRAME` (CScene.cpp::LWE_FBDUMP, writes .ppm), `LWE_FBPROFILE` (CScene.cpp::LWE_FBPROFILE; present variant CWallpaper.cpp::LWE_FBPROFILE), `LWE_MOUSE_POS` synthetic pointer pin (CScene.cpp::LWE_MOUSE_POS), `LWE_NOPARTICLES` (CScene.cpp::LWE_NOPARTICLES), `LWE_KILLLIGHT` (CScene.cpp::LWE_KILLLIGHT), `LWE_LIGHTDUMP` (CScene.cpp::LWE_LIGHTDUMP), `LWE_CROPOFF` (CImage.cpp::LWE_CROPOFF), `LWE_IMGDUMP` (CImage.cpp::LWE_IMGDUMP). Two unconditional diagnostics at `sLog.error` level: `LWE-DEBUG` projection dump in the CScene ctor (CScene.cpp::DEBUG) and `LWE-SCENEFB` for the first 3 frames (CScene.cpp::SCENEFB) - these log unconditionally, with no env gate. No capability here to lift; a porter would drop most of these.

**Tests (whole area)**: `src/WallpaperEngine/Testing/Cases/` contains BinaryParserHardening, CommandDispatcher, CommandSocket, Example (a `REQUIRE(true)` stub), InstrumentRegistry, MouseCoordinates, PointerMoveGate, PropertyParser, WebHelperStartupCost - none reference `Render/`. Nothing in this area has test coverage.

## 5-line summary

The fork turns the render core into a VRAM-conscious pipeline built on retained (LZ4-slimmed) pixel backing: after upload the uncompressed copy is freed and the LZ4 block is the canonical store, serving mip-residency expansion and the texcache hash. Scene rendering is resized end-to-end - canvas/view split, coverage-sized layer composites clamped to output-sizexLWE_CLAMPCOMPOSITES while the scene target clamps to output-sizexLWE_SSFACTOR, and a ping-pong composite FBO pool - while an HDR bloom ladder (RGBA16F targets, generated prefilter/down/up/combine passes) replaces the fixed 3-pass bloom when `bloom.hdr` is set. Textures get a capped-mip residency system (on by default, any LWE_TEXDETAIL other than auto opts out) with per-frame demand expansion, plus an offline BC7/BC4/BC5 texcache ingested by a new ispc shim; the CPU texture cache evicts sole-owner entries at rebuild/release points. Camera handling adds true perspective projection, scripted view overrides, zoom-corrected ortho, and a separate screen projection. Everything is env-flag-gated with default-on kill switches (LWE_FBOPOOL=0, LWE_NOFBOCOVERAGE, LWE_SSFACTOR=0, LWE_TEXCOMP=0, LWE_NOBLOOM), but none of it has test coverage, and two `sLog.error` diagnostics in CScene are always-on with no env gate.

---

# 4. Render objects: lighting, 3D, particles, text, shaders

- **What it does**: A new renderable object type for WE scene `model` objects. It parses the binary MDLV container directly (`CModel::loadMesh`, `src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Objects/CModel.cpp::loadMesh`): magic check at CModel.cpp::loadMesh, version digits at CModel.cpp::loadMesh, submesh records with 48-byte vertices (pos3+normal3+tangent4+uv2, u16 indices), with two alternate layouts - skinned tag `0x0180000f` -> stride 80/uv 72 (CModel.cpp::loadMesh) and tag 0 with MDLV version <16 -> stride 52/uv 44 (CModel.cpp::loadMesh). Submesh 0 uses the object material, submeshes 1..N use `extraMaterials` (CModel.cpp::extraMaterials); submeshes are optionally sorted translucent-last when `scene.transparentSorting` is on (CModel.cpp::loadMesh). Each submesh gets its own `Effects::CPass` with the material's first pass, forced `LIGHTING=1` combo if absent (CModel.cpp::LIGHTING), mesh attribs `a_Position@0, a_Normal@12, a_Tangent4@24, a_TexCoord@uvOffset` (CModel.cpp::setupPass), and a geometry callback that binds the submesh VAO, sets `glFrontFace(GL_CW)` and draws `GL_UNSIGNED_SHORT` triangles (CModel.cpp::GL_CW). Handles both perspective scenes (camera lookAt/projection, `CModel::updateMatrices` CModel.cpp::effectiveAngles) and ortho scenes with CParticle-style Y-flip conventions (CModel.cpp::updateMatrices), including per-object `perspective` models using scene fov + `perspectiveoverridefov` (CModel.cpp::perspectiveoverridefov). A bezier-keyframed `anglesAnimation` (`PropertyAnimation`, 3 channels at 30 fps) is evaluated in `effectiveAngles` (CModel.cpp::effectiveAngles, `bezierEase`/`evalChannel` CModel.cpp::bezierEase).
- **Where it lives**: fork-only `src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Objects/CModel.cpp` / `CModel.h` (class `CModel : CRenderable, ScriptableObject`, `CModel.h::CModel`). Instantiated in the fork-modified scene factory `CScene::dispatchObjectType`, `src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Wallpapers/CScene.cpp::ModelObject`. Data model `ModelObject`/`ModelObjectData` is fork-added in `src/WallpaperEngine/Data/Model/Object.h::ModelObjectData`.
- **Surface**: env vars - `LWE_TINTFIX=1|2` (TINTMASKALPHA combo off / force color constant, `CModel.cpp::LWE_TINTFIX`), `LWE_SPECFIX` (force roughness 1.0, CModel.cpp::LWE_SPECFIX), `LWE_LIGHTDUMP` (log LIGHTING define presence, CModel.cpp::LWE_LIGHTDUMP), `LWE_FRONTFACE=ccw` (CModel.cpp::LWE_FRONTFACE), `LWE_AUDIT` (CModel.cpp::LWE_AUDIT), `LWE_CAMPROBE` (CModel.cpp::LWE_CAMPROBE). Script properties registered: origin/scale/angles/visible/alpha/color (CModel.cpp::registerProperty). No CLI flag of its own, though each of these variables can also be set with `--debug` by the plain name `--help-debug` lists (modeltint, modelshine, scenedetails, frontface, audit and cameraprobe); the object renders only when a scene contains a `model` object.
- **Coupling**: new files + one factory branch in CScene + new `ModelObject` data struct (covered elsewhere). Reuses upstream `CPass`, `FBOProvider`, `Material` untouched. A porter needs: `CModel.*`, the `ModelObject` data model + parser entry, the CScene factory branch, and (for lit models) the LightingV1 stack below. Self-contained otherwise.
- **Tests**: none under `src/WallpaperEngine/Testing/Cases/`.
- **Uncertain**: the MDLV submesh-record layout (bounds skip CModel.cpp::loadMesh, header `2*sizeof(uint32_t)` skip CModel.cpp::loadMesh) is reconstructed from observation; correctness of the version<16 52-byte layout can't be verified from code alone.

### Puppet skeletal animation (PuppetModel)
- **What it does**: Fork-only MDL/MDLV puppet parser with skeleton and clip support. `PuppetModel::parse` (`src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Objects/PuppetModel.cpp::parse`) locates the skinned mesh block via a structured walk of vertex-attribute bit tags (PuppetModel.cpp::parse) with a stride-80 heuristic scan fallback (PuppetModel.cpp::parse), then parses the `MDLS` skeleton block (`parseSkeleton` PuppetModel.cpp::parseSkeleton; bind world-inverse per bone, D3D row-major matrices transposed by memcpy, `readFileMatrix` PuppetModel.cpp::readFileMatrix) and `MDLA` animation clips (`parseAnimations` PuppetModel.cpp::parseAnimations; loop/mirror/single modes, per-bone channels of 36-byte pos/rot/scale records). Mesh failure -> `nullopt`; skeleton/animation failure degrades to a static mesh (PuppetModel.cpp::parseSkeleton). `evaluateSkinning` (PuppetModel.cpp::evaluateSkinning) blends active layers (`rate`, `blend`) over rest pose with loop/mirror/single phase wrapping (PuppetModel.cpp::clipFrame) and linear key sampling; `skinPositions` (PuppetModel.cpp::skinPositions) does CPU 4-bone skinning. In CImage this replaces upstream's static puppet loader: `CImage::loadPuppetMesh` now calls `PuppetModel::parse` (`CImage.cpp::loadPuppetMesh`), binds `ImageAnimationLayer`s to clip ids (CImage.cpp::loadPuppetMesh), and `CImage::updatePuppetAnimation` (CImage.cpp::updatePuppetAnimation) re-skins and re-uploads the position VBO every frame (`uploadPuppetPositions` CImage.cpp::uploadPuppetPositions, `GL_DYNAMIC_DRAW`).
- **Where it lives**: fork-only `src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Objects/PuppetModel.{h,cpp}`; integration in modified upstream `CImage.cpp::loadPuppetMesh` and new members in `CImage.h::m_puppetScreenSpace` (`m_puppetModel`, `m_puppetLayers`, `m_puppetScreenSpace`, scratch vectors). Note: upstream already had a static puppet mesh loader (MDLV0021/0023, upstream `CImage.cpp::loadPuppetMesh`); the fork replaces that parser entirely.
- **Surface**: none - no env vars or flags; driven by the wallpaper's `puppet` model field + `animationLayers` (animation id, `visible`, `rate`, `blend`, `Object.h::blend`). `LWE_CROPOFF` is explicitly skipped for puppet models (`CImage.cpp::CImage`).
- **Coupling**: the parser itself is a self-contained module (only depends on Log + glm). The CImage integration replaces upstream `loadPuppetMesh`/`updatePuppetPositionBuffer` bodies and adds the screen-space puppet path (`m_puppetScreenSpace` set in `setupPasses`, `CImage.cpp::m_puppetScreenSpace`). A porter could take `PuppetModel.*` plus the CImage puppet block as one unit; the animation-layer data already exists upstream: `Object.h::ImageAnimationLayer` carried `id`/`rate`/`visible`/`blend`/`animation` before the fork, which adds only the `additive` field.
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: the inter-clip padding back-probing heuristic (PuppetModel.cpp::parseAnimations) and per-bone flags semantics (PuppetModel.cpp::parseSkeleton, "semantics unknown" in code comment) are heuristic by admission of the code itself.

### Light object (CLight) and the LightingV1 generated shader module
- **What it does**: Two halves. (a) `CLight` (`src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Objects/CLight.cpp`) is a scriptable, non-rendering scene object (`render()` is empty, CLight.cpp::render) that registers the light's `origin`, `angles`, `visible`, `color`, `intensity` DynamicValues so scripts can tick them (CLight.cpp::angles); its static members compute native-format light data: spot cone cosines (CLight.cpp::calculateSpotConeCosines), spot shadow view-projection (CLight.cpp::calculateSpotShadowViewProjection), camera-frustum-fitted directional shadow VP with texel snapping (CLight.cpp::calculateDirectionalShadowViewProjection), six-face point-light atlas VPs (CLight.cpp::calculatePointShadowViewProjections) and projection info (CLight.cpp::calculatePointShadowProjectionInfo), tube end position (CLight.cpp::calculateTubeEndPosition). (b) `ShaderUnit::generateLightingV1` (`src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Shaders/ShaderUnit.cpp::generateLightingV1`) replaces upstream's stub (which returned `vec3(0.0)` - upstream `ShaderUnit.cpp::generateLightingV1`) with a full GLSL module: uniform arrays for 4 point/spot/dir/tube lights + shadow atlas samplers, `lweShadowFeatureFactor`/`lwePointShadowFactor` sampling helpers, and a `PerformLighting_V1` that loops all four light types calling `ComputePBRLightShadow`/`ComputePBRLightShadowInfinite`. Injection is via the pre-existing `#require LightingV1` mechanism (`resolveRequireModule`, `ShaderUnit.cpp::resolveRequireModule`; upstream had the same hook at ShaderUnit.cpp::preprocessIncludes). On the CPU side, `CPass::refreshLightStage` (`src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Objects/Effects/CPass.cpp::classicLocalRadianceScale`) repacks `CScene::getLights()` into the `LightStageBlock` (`CPass.h::LightStageBlock`) each frame for programs that resolved the `lwe_Lit*`/`g_Lights*` uniforms (`m_usesSceneLights`, `CPass.cpp::m_usesSceneLights`), including the classic v2 `g_LightsPosition[4]`/`g_LightsColorPremultiplied[3]` interface with radius^2/divisor premultiply, per-renderable local-frame conversion (`toClassicLightSpace(Local)`, `classicLocalRadianceScale`, `CPass.cpp::classicLocalRadianceScale`) and falloff-exponent renorm (CPass.cpp::refreshLightStage).
- **Where it lives**: fork-only `CLight.{h,cpp}`; modified upstream `ShaderUnit.cpp` (`generateLightingV1`), `CPass.{h,cpp}` (`refreshLightStage`, uniform registration `CPass.cpp::refreshLightStage`, shadow-atlas bind `CPass.cpp::setupRenderTexture`, per-frame refresh `CPass.cpp::setupRenderTexture`), new virtuals on `CRenderable.h::toClassicLightSpace`. Scene side (the coupling target): `CScene::updateLights` `CScene.cpp::updateLights+` builds `m_lightState` from `Light` objects (types `lpoint/lspot/ltube/ldirectional`, CScene.cpp::m_lightState), factory branch CScene.cpp::updateLights, `SceneLight`/`MAX_LIGHTS=4`/`MAX_SHADOW_FEATURES=16` in `CScene.h::MAX_LIGHTS`.
- **Surface**: env `LWE_KILLLIGHT=<id>` drops one light (`CScene.cpp::LWE_KILLLIGHT`), `LWE_LIGHTDUMP` (uniform resolution + model pass logs, `CPass.cpp::LWE_LIGHTDUMP`, `CModel.cpp::LWE_LIGHTDUMP`), `LWE_CLASSICK` (default 16.0, range 0.01-1000) and `LWE_CLASSICEXP` (default 2.0, range 0.5-6) seed globals `g_LweClassicDivisor`/`g_LweFalloffExp` (read by Config, `Application/Config.cpp::LWE_CLASSICK`), also settable at runtime via socket command args `classic_k`/`classic_exp` (WallpaperApplication.cpp::g_LweClassicDivisor) and reported in status (WallpaperApplication.cpp::g_LweClassicDivisor). `LWE_NOSCREEN` disables the `g_Screen` uniform (`CPass.cpp::LWE_NOSCREEN`).
- **Coupling**: deeply woven. Touches upstream `ShaderUnit::generateLightingV1` body, `CPass::setupUniforms`/`render`/`setupRenderTexture`, adds pure-virtual-adjacent virtuals to `CRenderable` (with defaults, so non-breaking), and requires the whole fork CScene light/shadow stage (`SceneLight`, `ShadowStage`, `getLights()`, `getShadowStage()`, `getShadowAtlas()`). The GLSL module additionally calls `ComputePBRLightShadow(Infinite)`, which is defined nowhere in the fork tree - it must come from wallpaper-bundled shader includes, so the module only links in shaders that include WE's lighting headers. A porter must take CLight + CScene light snapshot + CPass staging + the generator together; the classic v2 interface also needs `CImage::toClassicLightSpaceLocal`/`classicLocalRadianceScale` (`CImage.cpp::toClassicLightSpaceLocal`) and `CPass::setClassicLocalFrame`/`setScreenViewProjectionMatrix` wiring in `CImage::setupPasses` (`CImage.cpp::setClassicLocalFrame`).
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: whether the premultiplier formula (`radius^2/g_LweClassicDivisor`, `CPass.cpp::refreshLightStage`) matches native WE output - it's a calibration dial with env/socket overrides, not derived from a spec in code. Whether `ComputePBRLightShadow` exists in workshop shader include files cannot be verified from this tree.

### Shadow mapping
- **What it does**: A depth-only shadow atlas shared by all lit passes. `CScene::setupShadowStage`/`renderShadowAtlas` (`CScene.cpp::renderShadowAtlas`) lay out a square atlas (grid of feature cells + 2x3 blocks per shadowed point light, CScene.cpp::setupShadowStage) and render each frame; only `CModel` objects cast shadows, via `CModel::renderShadow` (`CModel.cpp::renderShadow`) which lazily builds an inline `#version 330` depth-only program (CModel.cpp::renderShadow) and draws all submeshes with the light VP; the program and its VAO are freed with the model, and a compile or link failure latches the model out of shadow rendering (CModel.cpp::m_shadowFailed). Consumers: `CPass::setupRenderTexture` binds the atlas depth texture to unit 16 (`CPass.cpp::setupRenderTexture`) and the generated LightingV1 module samples it as `sampler2DShadow lwe_ShadowAtlas` with per-feature matrices/transforms and per-point six-face blocks (`ShaderUnit.cpp::generateLightingV1`). `CLight`'s static VP calculators (above) produce the matrices.
- **Where it lives**: fork-only `CLight.*`; modified `CModel.cpp::renderShadow`; fork-added CScene shadow stage (`CScene.cpp::setupShadowStage`, called from render at CScene.cpp::renderShadowAtlas); `CPass.cpp::setupRenderTexture`; generated GLSL in `ShaderUnit.cpp`.
- **Surface**: uniform `lwe_ShadowAtlas` fixed to texture unit 16 (`CPass.cpp::lwe_ShadowAtlas`). No dedicated kill switch; lights without `castShadow` get `enabled=0` (`CScene.cpp::stageShadowMatrices` region, feature assignment CScene.cpp::calculateSpotShadowViewProjection).
- **Coupling**: inseparable from the light stack (previous entry) plus CScene's atlas FBO with `ensureDepthTextureAttachment` (CScene.cpp::setupShadowStage). Porter takes it as part of the lighting bundle.
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: point-light face order is hard-coded to match `CalculateProjectedCoordsPoint` per comment (`CLight.cpp::CalculateProjectedCoordsPoint`) - that native function is not in this tree, so the claim rests on the comment.

### Particle semantics: playback rate, startTime, prewarm, child systems & events, instance overrides
- **What it does**: `CParticle::simulate` (CParticle.cpp::simulate) gates simulation on authored `startTime` (CParticle.cpp::m_startWall), applies the `rate` instance override as a playback-rate dilation of dt clamped to <=1 (CParticle.cpp::playbackRate), keeps a separate system clock `m_sysTime` for operators (CParticle.cpp::m_sysTime), and fast-forward "prewarms" 60s at 30Hz steps when `startTime>0` on root systems (CParticle.cpp::m_sysTime). Child particle systems (`Particle.children`, `Object.h::ParticleChild`) are built recursively in `setupChildren` (CParticle.cpp::setupChildren) with inherited multiplicative overrides `m_inhSize/Alpha/Lifetime/Speed/ColorN` captured in the delegating constructor (CParticle.h::m_inhSize). `emitAsChild` (CParticle.cpp::emitAsChild) implements `static` children (own start-time gate, `m_childStartWall`), `eventspawn`/`eventdeath` one-shot burst instances at parent particle positions (`spawnBurstInstance`), and `eventfollow` attachments that track a parent particle by stable `uid`, move their owned particles (and trail nodes) by anchor delta, copy the parent alpha into `followAlpha`, and respect per-instance budgets (`EmitContext{anchor,tag,budget,burst}`, `CParticle.h::EmitContext`). Parents publish per-frame spawn/death event lists (`recordSpawnRange`, `m_frameSpawns/m_frameDeaths`, `CParticle.h::CParticle`). Both emitters now take `EmitContext`, multiply rate by the `count` override at emission time (box: `CParticle.cpp` diff hunk ~CParticle.cpp::createBoxEmitter; sphere similar), and stamp `uid`/`ownerTag`. Pool sizing for event children is `maxCount x link.maxCount` capped at 4096 (CParticle.cpp::maxCount). Other semantic changes: emitter-origin de-scaling under `flags&1` (box CParticle.cpp::createBoxEmitter area), `worldSizeDivisor` size normalization, gravity de-scaled by object scale and drag halved (movement operator), turbulence operator rewritten (per-slot random speed, z-axis time), oscillatealpha frequency branch (`freqMin>=1` -> 60Hz-normalized cosine with `max(scaleMin, scaleMax*cos)`), oscillateposition converted from derivative-integration to absolute-offset deltas (`lastOffset`), control-point-attract threshold no longer halved, spritesheet frame clock changed to fractional wrap by default (`LWE_ANIMFRACTION=0` restores legacy), `SPRITESHEETBLEND=1` combo always set with SPRITESHEET (CParticle.cpp::SPRITESHEETBLEND), child parent-control-point mirroring (`updateTransformAndControlPoints`), and a pkg-workshop-specific dual `colorrandom` composition initializer (`createPkgDualColorRandomInitializer`, gated on `project.fromPackage`). Script properties registered for all instance overrides: alpha/size/lifetime/rate/speed/count/color/colorn (CParticle.cpp::colornAuthored).
- **Where it lives**: all in modified upstream `CParticle.{h,cpp}` - new members `CParticle.h::m_vboCapacity`; child ctor `CParticle.h::ParticleChild`; `EmitContext` `CParticle.h::EmitContext`. Depends on the fork-added `ParticleInstanceOverride.colornAuthored` and on the `definition` field of `ParticleChild` (`Object.h::ParticleChild`); `Particle.startTime/children` predate the fork and are newly consumed here.
- **Surface**: env kill switches/probes - `LWE_NOPREWARM` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_NOPREWARM), `LWE_ANIMFRACTION=0` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_ANIMFRACTION), `LWE_NOFOLLOWALPHA` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_NOFOLLOWALPHA), `LWE_NOCHILDRIDE` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_NOCHILDRIDE), `LWE_HIDESTPARENT` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_HIDESTPARENT), `LWE_BILLBOARD` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_BILLBOARD), `LWE_NOSPRITEVFLIP` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_NOSPRITEVFLIP), `LWE_TRAILMODE=exact` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_TRAILMODE), `LWE_PARTALLOC` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_PARTALLOC), `LWE_VELPROBE` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_VELPROBE), `LWE_SIZEPROBE` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_SIZEPROBE); runtime-toggleable instruments via `InstrumentRegistry`: `LWE_PARTSTATS`, `LWE_TWINKLEPROBE`, `LWE_ROPETRAILPROBE` (`Logging/InstrumentRegistry.cpp::WallpaperEngine::Logging`; settable via socket - `WallpaperApplication.cpp::handleApiCommand`), `LWE_NOPARTICLES` in the scene factory (`CScene.cpp::LWE_NOPARTICLES`) plus settings `general.disableParticles`.
- **Parallel simulation**: `CParticle::render` is split into `simulate` (clock, prewarm, emission, operators; no GL) and the draw (CParticle.cpp::simulate, CParticle.h::m_simulatedPass keys it to the driver pass so it runs once per pass wherever it is called from). `CScene::renderFrame` runs `simulate` for every root particle system on a worker pool right after the script tick and the animation tick, before any object renders (CScene.cpp::simulateParticles, Utils/WorkPool.cpp::WorkPool - fixed workers, min(8, cores - 2), a batch-and-wait `run`); `render` then only uploads and draws. Systems read settled property values and control points and write only their own state, so they are independent; instruments that print from inside `update` may interleave. Each task times itself and the scene keeps a smoothed serial-equivalent cost (CScene.h::m_particleSimCostUs); the pool is used only when that cost exceeds one millisecond (CScene.cpp::PARALLEL_COST_FLOOR_US), so light scenes simulate inline and pay nothing for the threads at any cap. `LWE_NOPARSIM` (CScene.cpp::LWE_NOPARSIM) forces the serial order for A/B. Measured on a six-emitter 150k-particle scene, three outputs: 39 -> 79 scene renders per second uncapped, 60.0 at cap 60.
- **Vertex build off the render thread**: the sprite vertex and index arrays are filled at the tail of `simulate` for the whole system tree (CParticle.cpp::buildSpriteTree, CParticle.cpp::buildSpriteVertices, keyed like the simulation on CParticle.h::m_builtPass), so the render thread only uploads and draws; a system rendered without a prior simulate builds them in place. Uploads of 256 KB and up orphan the buffer before `glBufferSubData` (CParticle.cpp::uploadParticleBuffer) so the driver never waits on the previous frame's draw; small buffers copy in place. Measured on the same six-emitter scene: 79 -> 115 scene renders per second uncapped, render thread 55% -> 26% busy.
- **Noise**: the turbulence operator and the vapor-stream direction sample `curlNoise` (NoiseUtils.h::curlNoise), now the curl of the analytic gradients of the three offset Perlin fields (NoiseUtils.h::perlinNoiseGrad, gradient table NoiseUtils.h::PERLIN_GRADS) in single precision: three gradient evaluations per particle instead of eighteen value evaluations through finite differences. Upstream's double-precision `perlinNoise` (NoiseUtils.h::perlinNoise) stays as the reference the test compares against. Measured on a six-emitter 150k-particle scene: scene renders 17 -> 39 per second uncapped.
- **Coupling**: contained in CParticle + its data model, but the file is heavily rewritten (~1900 diff lines); `EmitterFunc` signature changed (`CParticle.h::EmitterFunc`) so any out-of-tree emitter code breaks. Not liftable piecemeal - rate/startTime/prewarm could be cherry-picked alone (small), but children/events need the data model + EmitContext + emitters rewrite.
- **Tests**: `Testing/Cases/CurlNoise.cpp` pins the single-precision Perlin value to the double version and the analytic curl to the double-precision finite-difference curl of the same three fields. `Testing/Cases/CommandDispatcher.cpp::set-particles` covers the `set-particles` socket command (enable/disable), not semantics.
- **Uncertain**: `createPkgDualColorRandomInitializer`'s channel formulas are wallpaper-specific curve fitting - no in-code reference to validate against. The `rate` override is applied twice in effect: as dt dilation in simulate() AND `rate` is no longer folded into emitter rate (`const float rate = emitter.rate;` replacing upstream's `emitter.rate * override.rate`) - the net behavior change vs upstream is deliberate per code, but native parity is unverifiable.

### Trail/rope renderer rework + particle buffer pooling
- **What it does**: `renderRope` (`CParticle.cpp` diff ~CParticle.cpp::renderSprites+) is rewritten from "one strip over the live array" to multi-strip: each particle keeps a time-stamped `TrailNode` history (`CParticle.h::TrailNode`, recorded in `simCommon` with `m_trailLength` window and 4096-node cap), and per-particle strips are resampled (uniform segments, or `LWE_TRAILMODE=exact` tau-based times), then Catmull-Rom subdivided as before. Short `ropetrail` (length < 0.35) collapses to billboard sprite rendering under `LWE_BILLBOARD` (CParticle.cpp::LWE_BILLBOARD). GL upload switches from per-frame `glBufferData` re-spec to capacity-preallocated buffers with `glBufferSubData` (`uploadParticleBuffer`, CParticle.h::TrailNode; capacity set at CParticle.cpp::setupPass).
- **Where it lives**: `CParticle.cpp`/`CParticle.h` (TrailNode, `m_vboCapacity/m_eboCapacity` CParticle.h::m_vboCapacity).
- **Surface**: `LWE_TRAILMODE=exact`, `LWE_BILLBOARD`, `LWE_PARTALLOC` (pool/upload logging), `LWE_NOROPEUVFLIP` disables a rope-UV flip patch in the vertex shader source (`ShaderUnit.cpp::LWE_NOROPEUVFLIP`).
- **Coupling**: internal to CParticle; self-contained cherry-pick except that trail nodes interact with eventfollow anchor deltas (particles' trails are translated, `emitAsChild`).
- **Tests**: none.

### Text object support (CText fixes and features)
- **What it does**: CText existed upstream (FreeType single-quad text); the fork: (a) rasterizes at `pointSize * 4/3 * sceneH/1080` capped at 512px instead of scale-compensated sizing (`CText.cpp::computeEffectivePixelSize`); (b) decodes UTF-8 codepoints instead of bytes (`nextUtf8Codepoint`, CText.cpp::nextUtf8Codepoint, used at CText.cpp::rebuildTextureFrom+); (c) implements `limitwidth`/`maxwidth` truncation with optional ellipsis (CText.cpp::limitwidth); (d) applies horizontal/vertical alignment offsets from font metrics (`computeTextAlignmentOffset`, CText.cpp::computeTextAlignmentOffset, applied to quad verts CText.cpp::uploadQuadVertices); (e) walks the parent chain (depth <=8, rotation/scale/origin compose) for placement (CText.cpp::render); (f) renders Y-flip-corrected via `cam.getScreenProjection()` (ortho scenes also multiply lookAt) and explicitly binds the scene FBO, disables depth/cull, alpha-mask off (CText.cpp::GL_DEPTH_TEST). Scripted text re-rasterizes whenever the script's output string changes (same as upstream; the code comment groups scripted text with its "Phase 2" font-loading deferral, but the shipped code already implements it, so the comment is stale on that point).
- **Where it lives**: modified upstream `src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Objects/CText.{cpp,h}` (new member `m_alignOffset`, `CText.h::m_alignOffset`).
- **Surface**: `LWE_LIGHTDUMP` enables a one-shot text dump (`CText.cpp::LWE_LIGHTDUMP`). No flag of its own, though the variable can also be set with `--debug` by the plain name `--help-debug` lists (scenedetails); driven by `Text` object properties (alignment, verticalalign, maxwidth, limitwidth, limituseellipsis).
- **Coupling**: self-contained within CText; depends only on `Camera::getScreenProjection`/`isOrthogonal` (covered elsewhere). Easy cherry-pick.
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: alignment math uses `lineCount=1` hard-coded (CText.cpp::rebuildTextureFrom) - multi-line vertical alignment is approximate by construction.

### CImage compositing: FBO coverage, shared composites, float composites, composition layers
- **What it does**: (a) FBO coverage: composite FBOs are sized to at least the layer's on-screen footprint (`|size*scale|` clamped to scene, `CImage.cpp::LWE_NOFBOCOVERAGE`), then `scene.clampToEffectCap` with the effect factor `LWE_CLAMPCOMPOSITES` (a number at most 4, 0 or below off); default on, `LWE_NOFBOCOVERAGE` disables, `LWE_FBOCOVERAGE` logs. (b) Shared composites: `scene.leaseCompositePair(id, ...)` (CImage.cpp::leaseCompositePair) hands out pooled ping-pong FBOs, and `render()` keeps rendering invisible images whose composite is shared (`isCompositeShared`, CImage.cpp::isCompositeShared); when rendering into a composition the final pass leaves alpha writable (CImage.cpp::isRenderingToComposition vs upstream's unconditional `GL_FALSE`). (c) Float composites: format is `RGBA16161616f` when `scene.isHdrBloom()` else ARGB8888 (CImage.cpp::isHdrBloom). (d) Composition layers: `isCompositionLayer()` (`models/util/composelayer.json`, CImage.cpp::isCompositionLayer) with authored children allocates a scene-sized `_rt_compositionLayer_<id>` FBO and aliases `_rt_FullFrameBuffer` to it on the object (CImage.cpp::isCompositionLayer); `CImage` also exposes `copiesCompositionBackground()` (CImage.cpp::copiesCompositionBackground) and `cursorLocalPosition()` (rotation-aware hit test, CImage.cpp::cursorLocalPosition). Also fork-added: shape-layer quad geometry from `point0..3` constants (`m_isShape`, `applyShapeGeometry`, CImage.cpp::applyShapeGeometry, additive blending CImage.cpp::setup), `buildScreenViewProjection` with per-object perspective support (CImage.cpp::buildScreenViewProjection), rewritten parallax term (CImage.cpp::updateScreenSpacePosition), and effect-build failure rollback (CImage.cpp::setup). The magenta-neon tint pass (CImage.cpp::isMagentaNeonTint), NPOT texture UV clamping and `passthrough` copy-projection handling all predate the fork. `getColor4()` now returns colorxbrightness by value (CImage.cpp::getColor4).
- **Where it lives**: modified upstream `CImage.{cpp,h}`; the pooling/sharing/compostion state lives in fork CScene (`leaseCompositePair`, `isCompositeShared`, `isRenderingToComposition`, `hasAuthoredChildren`, `isHdrBloom`, `clampToCap`, `clampToEffectCap` - `CScene.h::isRenderingToComposition`), and `CRenderable::detectTexture` now resolves `_rt_`/`_alias_` names through the per-object FBOProvider chain (`this->find`, `CRenderable.cpp::find`) so the `_rt_FullFrameBuffer` alias works per-subtree.
- **Surface**: `LWE_NOFBOCOVERAGE`, `LWE_FBOCOVERAGE`, `LWE_CROPOFF=1|2` (CImage.cpp::LWE_NOFBOCOVERAGE), `LWE_IMGDUMP`, `LWE_IMGPROBE`, `LWE_CURSORDBG`, `LWE_PASSPROBE[=<id>|final]` + `LWE_PASSPROBE_DUMP` (`CPass.cpp::LWE_PASSPROBE`). No config keys.
- **Coupling**: woven into CImage's constructor/setupPasses/render and depends on several new CScene APIs - a porter needs the CScene composite-pool half (covered elsewhere) plus `FBOProvider::alias` (`src/WallpaperEngine/Render/FBOProvider.h::alias`, fork-changed file). The shape-geometry and magenta-tint blocks are individually excisable; coverage sizing is a ~15-line block.
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: `isMagentaNeonTint` thresholds (CImage.cpp::isMagentaNeonTint) and the tint compat pass are targeted wallpaper workarounds; "shape" detection keys on a fork-specific material filename `materials/wpenginelinux_shape.json` (CImage.cpp::applyShapeGeometry) whose origin is not verified here.

### Sound-object gain
- **What it does**: Each `AudioStream` created for a sound object now gets the authored object volume clamped to [0,1] (`CSound.cpp::LWE_NOOBJVOL`); upstream set only repeat mode. Kill switch `LWE_NOOBJVOL` forces 1.0.
- **Where it lives**: modified upstream `CSound.cpp::load` (header unchanged). Uses upstream `AudioStream::setVolume`.
- **Surface**: env `LWE_NOOBJVOL` (CSound.cpp::load); data: `Sound.volume`.
- **Coupling**: 4 lines, trivially cherry-pickable.
- **Tests**: none.

### Texture animation playback control (script-driven pause/play/frame)
- **What it does**: `CRenderable` gains a `TextureAnimationPlayback` state (`CRenderable.h::baseTime`) with pause (freeze clock at current g_Time), play (reset to free-run), and setFrame/getFrame/getFrameCount/isPlaying (`CRenderable.cpp::baseTime`). `CPass::resolveTextureAnimationState` honors it: frame override picks the frame directly; paused uses the frozen `baseTime` instead of driver render time; animation time is now summed from the texture's own frames with a <=0 guard (`CPass.cpp::baseTime`). Exposed to scene scripts through the QuickJS adapter (`ScriptableObjectAdapter.cpp::pauseTextureAnimation`, magic 0-5).
- **Where it lives**: modified upstream `CRenderable.{h,cpp}`, `CPass.cpp`; adapter in `src/WallpaperEngine/Scripting/Adapters/ScriptableObjectAdapter.cpp` (covered in the script-engine chapter).
- **Surface**: script functions only; `LWE_ANIMSTATS` instruments frame advance rates (`CPass.cpp::LWE_ANIMSTATS`).
- **Coupling**: small and additive; porter needs CRenderable block + the CPass frame-selection hunk + adapter entry.
- **Tests**: none.

### Shader pipeline compatibility layer (ShaderUnit/Shader/GLSLContext)
- **What it does**: A set of source-rewrites applied while assembling workshop GLSL: `preprocessGlobalConsts` demotes global `const` decls initialized from non-constant expressions into `#define`s wrapped in the declared type constructor, and adds explicit type constructors for function-scope ones (HLSL narrowing -> GLSL legality) (`ShaderUnit.cpp::preprocessGlobalConsts`); a sign flip in the skylight/ambient mix (`vec3(0,1,0)`->`vec3(0,-1,0)`, ShaderUnit.cpp::preprocess); rope UV V-flip for `genericropeparticle` (ShaderUnit.cpp::genericropeparticle); `g_LWEAxisComp` scale-compensation injection into `genericparticle` vertex shaders (ShaderUnit.cpp::g_LWEAxisComp); `applyTextureResolutionSwizzleCompatibility` adds `.xy` when `g_TextureNResolution` (vec4) is combined with vec2 (ShaderUnit.cpp::applyTextureResolutionSwizzleCompatibility); `paintdefaultcolor` sampler metadata creates 1x1 solid-color default textures (`m_paintDefaultColors` ShaderUnit.cpp::paintdefaultcolor, consumed in `CPass.cpp::setupTextureUniforms` via `SolidColorTexture`, `CPass.cpp::SolidColorTexture`); component-combo enabling for `METALLIC_MAP`/`ROUGHNESS_MAP`/`REFLECTION_MAP`/`EMISSIVE_MAP` from the mask texture's painted-component header bits (ShaderUnit.cpp::m_textureFlags, see "PBR mask component combos" below; until 2026-09-13 this was a constant-based heuristic that also enabled `EMISSIVE_MAP` unconditionally); float and numeric-string combo defaults now parse instead of throwing (ShaderUnit.cpp::parseComboConfiguration); combo/parameter JSON uses `parseLenient` (ShaderUnit.cpp::parseComboConfiguration); `Shader`/`ShaderUnit` constructors thread a new `materialConstants` map (pass-level constants) through to uniform binding (`Shader.cpp::materialConstants`, `CPass.cpp::setupShaders`); FOG combos `FOG_DIST/FOG_HEIGHT/FOG_COMPUTED` derived from scene fog (`CPass.cpp::FOG_DIST`); texture uniforms now registered for units 0-15 (`CPass.cpp::setupTextureUniforms`); uniform uploads became count-aware for vec3/vec4/mat4 arrays (`CPass.cpp::setupRenderUniforms`); include expansion is bounded per file and malformed directives are dropped (ShaderUnit.cpp::MAX_INCLUDE_EXPANSIONS); a glslang failure throws with its log instead of returning empty sources, and translated pairs are cached by exact source text within a byte budget (GLSLContext.cpp::MAX_TRANSLATION_BYTES).
- **Where it lives**: modified upstream `ShaderUnit.{cpp,h}`, `Shader.{cpp,h}`, `GLSLContext.cpp`, `CPass.{cpp,h}`.
- **Surface**: `LWE_FORCECOMBO=NAME=value` (global combo override, `ShaderUnit.cpp::LWE_FORCECOMBO`), `LWE_SHADERDUMP` (dump failing fragment source, `GLSLContext.cpp::LWE_SHADERDUMP`), `LWE_SHADERDUMP_MATCH=<substr>` (write assembled units to `~/.local/state/lwe/probes/`, `ShaderUnit.cpp::LWE_SHADERDUMP_MATCH`), `LWE_NOROPEUVFLIP`, `LWE_AUDIT`, `LWE_UNIFDUMP`/`LWE_UNIFVALS` (`CPass.cpp::LWE_UNIFDUMP`), `LWE_NOSCREEN`.
- **Coupling**: `Shader` ctor gained a parameter - single call site (`CPass.cpp::setupShaders`), so contained. The GLSL patches are sequential string rewrites inside `ShaderUnit::preprocess`/`compile`; individually excisable but order-sensitive. `parseLenient` is a data-layer dependency.
- **Tests**: none.
- **Uncertain**: the ambient sign flip and axis-compensation rewrites are calibrated to specific wallpapers; no in-code reference for native correctness.

### Pass uniform and attribute ownership

- **What it does**: Upstream's `CPass` registered every uniform and attribute with `new` into three containers of raw pointers that the destructor never walked, heap-copied the payload of by-value uniforms, and on a same-name re-registration freed the entry but not that copy. A pass is torn down on every wallpaper switch, so a playlist leaked a few dozen entries per pass per rotation. The reference-uniform path also deleted a same-named plain entry without erasing its map slot, leaving a freed pointer the render loop would walk. The fork holds the entries in owning pointers so replacement and teardown free them (`CPass.h::release`), gives a by-value entry a release function for its payload, and has each registration path erase the name from the other map (`CPass.cpp::payload`).
- **Where it lives**: modified `CPass.{h,cpp}` only, the registration templates and the three containers.
- **Surface**: none.
- **Coupling**: self-contained; consumers already reach the entries through pointer access.
- **Tests**: none.

---

**Area summary (5 lines)**: The fork adds full 3D scene-object support: a new `CModel` renders WE `model` objects by parsing MDLV meshes directly (`CModel.cpp::loadMesh`), and `PuppetModel` upgrades upstream's static puppet loader to CPU-skinned skeletal animation with loop/mirror/single clips (`PuppetModel.cpp::parse`, `CImage.cpp::updatePuppetAnimation`). Scene lighting is implemented end-to-end: a scriptable `CLight` object, a CScene light/shadow stage, and `ShaderUnit::generateLightingV1` (`ShaderUnit.cpp::generateLightingV1`) replacing upstream's zero-stub with a PBR lighting + shadow-atlas module injected via `#require LightingV1`, fed per-frame by `CPass::refreshLightStage` (`CPass.cpp::refreshLightStage`). Particles gain playback rate/startTime/prewarm, recursive child systems with eventspawn/eventdeath/eventfollow instance semantics, per-particle trail ribbons, and pooled GL buffers - all inside a heavily rewritten `CParticle`. CImage gets scale-aware FBO coverage, shared/float composite pools, composition-layer FBO aliasing, shape geometry and perspective layers; CText gains UTF-8, alignment, width-limiting and parent-chain placement; CSound honors authored volume (env kill switch `LWE_NOOBJVOL`). Everything is controlled by env vars and runtime-toggleable instruments (`LWE_*`, `InstrumentRegistry`), with essentially no test coverage under `Testing/Cases/` for this area; the lighting stack is the most deeply woven (CScene + CPass + ShaderUnit + CRenderable virtuals), while CText/CSound/texture-animation control are easy cherry-picks.

---

# 5. Data layer, binary parsers, filesystem


### Lenient JSON parsing pipeline (`parseLenient`)
- **What it does**: `JSON::parseLenient` first tries a strict nlohmann parse with `ignore_comments=true`; on `parse_error` it retries after blanking out `//` and `/* */` comments (string/escape aware, newlines preserved) and dropping commas whose next non-whitespace char is `]` or `}` (src/WallpaperEngine/Data/JSON.cpp::parseLenient, helpers `stripComments` at JSON.cpp::stripComments and `stripTrailingCommas` at JSON.cpp::stripTrailingCommas). Declared at src/WallpaperEngine/Data/JSON.h::parseLenient. This lets the fork read WE-authored JSON that carries comments and trailing commas, which upstream's bare `JSON::parse` rejects.
- **Where it lives**: modified upstream files src/WallpaperEngine/Data/JSON.cpp / JSON.h. Call sites swapped from `JSON::parse` to `parseLenient`: src/WallpaperEngine/Data/Parsers/EffectParser.cpp::parseLenient, MaterialParser.cpp::parseLenient, ModelParser.cpp::parseLenient, WallpaperParser.cpp::parseLenient, ObjectParser.cpp::parseLenient, plus new recursive particle-child loading that calls it directly at ObjectParser.cpp::parseLenient (particle definition files). It is also used at src/WallpaperEngine/Render/Shaders/ShaderUnit.cpp::parseLenient, src/WallpaperEngine/Application/WallpaperApplication.cpp::parseLenient, src/WallpaperEngine/Application/ApplicationContext.cpp::parseLenient, src/WallpaperEngine/WebBrowser/CEF/WPSchemeHandlerFactory.cpp::parseLenient.
- **Surface**: no flags/env; always on at those call sites. On the fallback path it logs `JSON strict parse failed - retrying with trailing commas stripped (WE-lenient)` (JSON.cpp::parseLenient).
- **Coupling**: low. `parseLenient` is an additive free function; call-site changes are one-liners. A porter needs JSON.cpp/JSON.h plus the call-site swaps they want. `EffectParser::load` and `MaterialParser::load` additionally throw on empty file content (EffectParser.cpp::readString, MaterialParser.cpp::load) - separate micro-hardening, same hunk.
- **Tests**: none in src/WallpaperEngine/Testing/Cases/.
